# Content repairs

Every change to the AI-written content, newest first. Each entry says what was wrong, the source used, and who approved it.

## 202611060311-rev108-arakhin-11-20

Approved: Drafted overnight Nov 5–6 2026 by Claude from a line-by-line check against the Gemara and Rashi on Sefaria (each finding re-checked before applying); David asked for fixes to go live overnight. Awaiting review by a rav.

- **Arakhin 13a, step 1** (confusionAlert): The whole point of the ברייתא is that יהויריב's watch was not in the Second בית המקדש; the alert says it served there in second place. Source: Arakhin 12b משמרתו של יהויריב בשניה מי הואי; 13a שאפילו יהויריב ראש משמרות עולה; Rashi 13a s.v. וכן חרים: ויהויריב שוב לא עלה.
  - was: It was still a watch, but in the Second בית המקדש it ranked behind ידעיה.
  - now: The prophets only ruled that if יהויריב ever came up, it would rank behind ידעיה. יהויריב never did come up, so it did not serve in the Second בית המקדש at all.
- **Arakhin 13a, step 3** (whatsHappening): The difficulty was that the count leaves six years over, so the destruction falls in the שמיטה year itself; years that divided evenly would give the year after שמיטה, the opposite. Source: Arakhin 12b פשו להו שית הוה ליה בשיתא בשבוע; Rashi 12b s.v. הוה ליה בשיתא: ובשביעית עצמה חרב.
  - was: when its 420 years divide evenly into שמיטה cycles?
  - now: when counting its 420 years puts the destruction in a שמיטה year?
- **Arakhin 11b, step 1** (hebrewStepName): This is the second half of חנניא's source from 11a step 29, which is labelled מימרא; a second half takes the first half's label across the amud break (rule 5). Source: Arakhin 11a-b חנניא בן אחי רבי יהושע אמר מהכא ... על עסקי קול; rulebook rule 5.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: מימרא
- **Arakhin 11b, step 7** (hebrewStepName): It rules out a possible reading of זר (אילימא זר ממש, הכתיב חדא זימנא); no proof is being pushed aside, so it is an objection, not a Rejection (rule 2). Source: Arakhin 11b מאי זר? אילימא זר ממש הכתיב חדא זימנא; rulebook rule 2.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Arakhin 12a, step 14** (hebrewStepName): The ברייתא of the exile dates (דאמר מר גלו בשבע...) is quoted to back up the challenge to רבינא in step 13, so it is part of the Challenge, not a Proof (rule 1). Source: Arakhin 12a אי הכי בעשרים וחמש שנה? עשרים ושש הויין! דאמר מר...; rulebook rule 1.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Arakhin 12b, step 7** (hebrewStepName): The ברייתא of seventeen יובלות is the source of the challenge in step 6 against the רבי יהודה answer (אי רבי יהודה בראשונה לא משכחת לה! דתניא...), so it is a Challenge (rule 1). Source: Arakhin 12b אי רבי יהודה בראשונה לא משכחת לה! דתניא: שבעה עשר יובלות; rulebook rule 1.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Arakhin 13a, step 9** (hebrewStepName): This is the second half of the answer to שבע שכיבשו מנלן (step 7), begun in step 8 (תשובה); an answer that quotes a source is a Response, and a second half takes the first half's label (rules 5, 7). Source: Arakhin 13a שבע שכיבשו מנלן? דקאמר כלב... ואמר מר... אישתכח דשבע כיבשו; rulebook rules 5, 7.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: תשובה
- **Arakhin 13b, step 18** (hebrewStepName): Continues רבי יהודה's ברייתא, quoted to challenge the משנה (לימא מתניתין דלא כרבי יהודה, דתניא...); every step quoting that source is a Challenge (rule 1). Source: Arakhin 13b לימא מתניתין דלא כרבי יהודה דתניא...; rulebook rule 1.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: קשיא
- **Arakhin 13b, step 19** (hebrewStepName): Same ברייתא of רבי יהודה quoted against the משנה (rule 1). Source: Arakhin 13b של עולם הבא עשר...; rulebook rule 1.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: קשיא
- **Arakhin 13b, step 20** (hebrewStepName): Same ברייתא of רבי יהודה quoted against the משנה; its last verse completes the challenge, not a proof (rule 1). Source: Arakhin 13b ואומר: הודו לה׳ בכנור בנבל עשור...!; rulebook rule 1.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Arakhin 14a, step 21** (hebrewStepName): This ברייתא is brought (דתניא) as the basis of the challenge in step 20 against assigning the tree ברייתא to רבי שמעון, so it belongs to the Challenge (rule 1). Source: Arakhin 14a ואי רבי שמעון ליזיל בתר פדיון... דהא שמעינן ליה... דתניא; rulebook rule 1.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Arakhin 14a, step 22** (hebrewStepName): Continuation of the same ברייתא quoted for the challenge (rule 1). Source: Arakhin 14a דברי רבי יהודה ורבי שמעון; rulebook rule 1.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Arakhin 14a, step 23** (hebrewStepName): Continuation of the same ברייתא quoted for the challenge (rule 1); the challenge goes on through 14b steps 1-3. Source: Arakhin 14a רבי מאיר אומר: מנין ללוקח שדה מאביו...; rulebook rule 1.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: קשיא
- **Arakhin 14b, step 1** (hebrewStepName): End of the same ברייתא (רבי מאיר's verse) quoted as part of the challenge against the רבי שמעון answer; continuation steps take the Challenge label (rules 1, 5). Source: Arakhin 14b תלמוד לומר אם את שדה מקנתו...; rulebook rules 1, 5.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: קשיא
- **Arakhin 14b, step 3** (hebrewStepName): אלא לאו משום דאזיל בתר פדיון is the inference that completes the challenge to the answer of 14a step 17 (that the tree ברייתא is רבי שמעון), not a Proof of a position (rules 1, 2). Source: Arakhin 14b אלא לאו משום דאזיל בתר פדיון; 14a ואי רבי שמעון ליזיל בתר פדיון; rulebook rules 1, 2.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Arakhin 14b, step 4** (hebrewStepName): רב נחמן בר יצחק answers a Challenge (to the רבי שמעון answer), so it is a Resolution; Rejection is only for pushing aside a Proof (rule 2). Source: Arakhin 14b אמר רב נחמן בר יצחק: רבי יהודה ורבי שמעון קרא אשכחו ודרוש; rulebook rule 2.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: תירוץ
- **Arakhin 14b, step 5** (hebrewStepName): No שאלה precedes it; it is the second half of רב נחמן בר יצחק's answer in step 4 (Rashi s.v. אם כן) and takes that step's label (rules 5, 6). Source: Arakhin 14b אם כן לכתוב רחמנא... מאי משדה; Rashi s.v. א״כ; rulebook rules 5, 6.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: תירוץ
- **Arakhin 15a, step 1** (hebrewStepName): This is the second half of רב זירא's answer to the challenge 'why pay beyond the fifty?' (labelled תירוץ on 14b), so it takes the same label (rulebook rule 5); it is not a Proof. Source: Arakhin 14b-15a: אמר רבי זירא... אילו באו עליה שנים; 14b step 30 is תירוץ.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: תירוץ
- **Arakhin 15a, step 2** (hebrewStepName): אביי objects to רב זירא's answer, not to a proof; an objection to an answer is a Challenge (rule 2). Source: Arakhin 15a: אמר ליה אביי: אי הכי, גבי עבד נמי יאמרו.

## 202611060303-rev103-berakhot-60-64

Approved: Drafted overnight Nov 5–6 2026 by Claude from a line-by-line check against the Gemara and Rashi on Sefaria (each finding re-checked before applying); David asked for fixes to go live overnight. Awaiting review by a rav.

- **Berakhot 60a, step 1** (hebrewStepName): This inference is the second half of the first version of the רב הונא / רבי יוחנן dispute (labelled מימרא on 59b); rule 5: a second half takes its first half's label and a Conclusion is never in the middle. Source: Berakhot 60a: מכלל דכי קנה וחזר וקנה דברי הכל אין צריך לברך; 59b last steps are מימרא; rulebook rule 5.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 60a, step 3** (hebrewStepName): The inference closes the second version and is immediately challenged by מיתיבי, so it cannot be a Conclusion (rule 5). Source: Berakhot 60a: מכלל דכי יש לו וקנה דברי הכל צריך לברך. מיתיבי...; rulebook rule 5.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 60a, step 17** (hebrewStepName): רב's reading of ואחר is the content of רב יוסף's objection (מתיב רב יוסף) against the משנה; a source quoted to attack is a Challenge, including its continuation (rule 1). Source: Berakhot 60a: ולא מהני רחמי? מתיב רב יוסף ... מיד נהפכה לבת; rulebook rule 1.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Berakhot 60a, step 17** (whatsHappening): Matches the label fix: the step is part of an objection, not a proof. Source: Berakhot 60a: מתיב רב יוסף.
  - was: This is the proof behind רב יוסף's objection.
  - now: This is the content of רב יוסף's objection.
- **Berakhot 60a, step 18** (hebrewStepName): אין מזכירין מעשה נסים answers רב יוסף's objection (a Challenge), so it is a Resolution; Rejection is only for pushing aside a proof (rule 2). Step 19, the second answer, is already תירוץ. Source: Berakhot 60a: אין מזכירין מעשה נסים. ואיבעית אימא...; rulebook rule 2.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: תירוץ
- **Berakhot 60a, step 18** (stepSummary): The step answers רב יוסף's objection; it is not rejecting a proof. Source: Berakhot 60a: מתיב רב יוסף ... אין מזכירין מעשה נסים.
  - was: The גמרא rejects the proof: we do not bring proofs from miracles.
  - now: The גמרא answers the challenge: we do not bring proofs from miracles.
- **Berakhot 60a, step 18** (whatsHappening): Same as above. Source: Berakhot 60a.
  - was: The גמרא pushes back on רב יוסף's proof.
  - now: The גמרא answers רב יוסף's objection.
- **Berakhot 60a, step 18** (relationToPreviousStep): Same as above. Source: Berakhot 60a.
  - was: Rejects the proof brought in Steps 16 and 17.
  - now: Answers the challenge raised in Steps 16 and 17.
- **Berakhot 60a, step 40** (hebrewStepName): אביי objects to the wording of a ברייתא's prayer; no proof is being pushed aside, so per rule 2 this is a Challenge (as the parallel אביי objection in step 47 is labelled). Source: Berakhot 60a: אמר אביי לא לימא איניש הכי דלא לפתח פומיה לשטן; rulebook rule 2.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Berakhot 60b, step 2** (hebrewStepName): אביי objects to the wording of the bathroom prayer and replaces it; nothing is a proof, so per rule 2 this is a Challenge (same as אביי's identical objections on 60a). Source: Berakhot 60b: אמר אביי לא לימא איניש הכי דלמא שבקי ליה ואזלי; rulebook rule 2.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Berakhot 60b, step 22** (hebrewStepName): The רבי עקיבא story is introduced with כי הא as an illustration of the habit just taught; story lines and illustrations are Statements (rule 8). Source: Berakhot 60b: כי הא דרבי עקיבא דהוה קאזיל באורחא; rulebook rule 8.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 60b, step 23** (hebrewStepName): Continuation of the illustrative story (rule 8). Source: Berakhot 60b: אזל ובת בדברא...; rulebook rule 8.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 60b, step 24** (hebrewStepName): Continuation of the illustrative story (rule 8). Source: Berakhot 60b: ביה בליליא אתא גייסא...; rulebook rule 8.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 60b, step 24** (deeperAnalysis): Matches the label fix: the story is brought with כי הא as an illustration, not as a proof. Source: Berakhot 60b: כי הא דרבי עקיבא.
  - was: This proves the teaching from step 21.
  - now: This illustrates the teaching from step 21.
- **Berakhot 61a, step 1** (hebrewStepName): הכל לטובה is the second half of רבי עקיבא's sentence from the story on 60b; the second half takes the first half's label across the amud break (rule 5), and the story lines are Statements (rule 8). Source: Berakhot 60b-61a: כל מה שעושה הקב"ה / הכל לטובה; rulebook rules 5 and 8.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 61b, step 15** (hebrewStepName): פפוס's 'are you not afraid' is labelled קשיא (an objection inside a story); the reply to a Challenge is a Resolution, not a Response (rules 3 and 6). Source: Berakhot 61b: אמר לו אמשול לך משל...; rulebook rules 3 and 6.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: תירוץ
- **Berakhot 61b, step 16** (hebrewStepName): The fish's reply is the middle of רבי עקיבא's parable answering פפוס; it pushes aside no proof, so Rejection is wrong (rule 2), and as part of the answer it takes the answer's label (rule 5). Source: Berakhot 61b: אמרו לו אתה הוא שאומרים עליך פקח שבחיות...; rulebook rules 2 and 5.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: תירוץ
- **Berakhot 61b, step 17** (hebrewStepName): The application of the parable is רבי עקיבא's answer to פפוס's challenge, so it is a Resolution (rule 6). Source: Berakhot 61b: אף אנחנו עכשיו...; rulebook rule 6.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: תירוץ
- **Berakhot 62a, step 27** (deeperAnalysis): Rashi explains the leniency as being for the sake of guarding the טהרות (no worry that someone impure enters while they step out), not that going far would bring the workers into contact with impurity. Source: Berakhot 62a בטהרות הקלו; Rashi s.v. יוצאין מפתח בית הבד (ואין חוששין שמא בתוך כך יכנס עם הארץ ויגע) and s.v. בטהרות הקלו (בשביל שמירת טהרות).
  - was: instead of going far away, where they might come into contact with impurity
  - now: instead of going far away and leaving the טהרות unguarded
- **Berakhot 62b, step 8** (hebrewStepName): רבי אבא quotes the משנה to attack רב ספרא's conduct; a source quoted to attack is a Challenge (rulebook rule 1), and the next steps defend רב ספרא against it. Source: Berakhot 62b לאו הכי תנן... אלמא לאו אורח ארעא הוא; Rashi s.v. ובית הכסא של כבוד (ואת אמרת ליעול מר).
  - was: ראיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Berakhot 62b, step 8** (title): Title names the step as a proof; it is part of רבי אבא's challenge. Source: Berakhot 62b לאו הכי תנן.
  - was: Proof from the משנה about the honorable בית הכסא in the בית המקדש
  - now: רבי אבא's source: the honorable בית הכסא in the בית המקדש
- **Berakhot 62b, step 9** (hebrewStepName): 'אלמא לאו אורח ארעא הוא' is the second half of רבי אבא's challenge and is immediately answered (והוא סבר מסוכן הוא), so it is not a Conclusion (rules 1 and 5). Source: Berakhot 62b אלמא לאו אורח ארעא הוא. והוא סבר מסוכן הוא.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: קשיא
- **Berakhot 62b, step 9** (title): Title names the step as a Conclusion; it is the end of רבי אבא's challenge, which is answered next. Source: Berakhot 62b.
  - was: Conclusion: speaking from the bathroom is not proper conduct
  - now: So speaking from the bathroom is not proper conduct
- **Berakhot 62b, step 13** (hebrewStepName): רבי אלעזר applying 'ואתן אדם תחתיך' to the Roman (קרי עליה) is a line of the story, not a source brought to support any disputed position (rule 8). Source: Berakhot 62b קרי עליה רבי אלעזר: ואתן אדם תחתיך.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 62b, step 48** (hebrewStepName): The ברייתא is quoted as the second half of רבא's answer to the challenge 'נילף מקפנדריא' (תנא יליף ממנעל... מאי היא? דתניא); an answer quoting a source is a Resolution (rules 5 and 7). Source: Berakhot 62b אמר ליה: תנא יליף ממנעל... מאי היא? דתניא; Rashi s.v. תנא יליף.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: תירוץ
- **Berakhot 63a, step 7** (hebrewStepName): The פסוק answers the question 'ומנין שאין עונין אמן במקדש' (step 6, שאלה); an answer citing a פסוק is a Response, not a Proof (rule 7). Source: Berakhot 63a ומנין שאין עונין אמן במקדש? שנאמר קומו ברכו.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: תשובה
- **Berakhot 63a, step 7** (title): Title names the step as a proof; it is the Response to the question of step 6. Source: Berakhot 63a.
  - was: Proof from the פסוק in נחמיה
  - now: The source: the פסוק in נחמיה
- **Berakhot 63a, step 8** (hebrewStepName): Continuation of the same answer from the same פסוק (יכול... תלמוד לומר); the second half takes the first half's label (rule 5). Source: Berakhot 63a יכול כל הברכות כולן תהא להן תהלה אחת.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: תשובה
- **Berakhot 63a, step 8** (deeperAnalysis): Rashi explains the hava amina as answering ברוך שם כבוד מלכותו once, after the last blessing, not a response like אמן (the whole point is that אמן is not said there). Source: Berakhot 63a תהא להן תהלה אחת; Rashi s.v. על כל הברכות (בסוף ברכה אחרונה), s.v. תהלה אחת (שיענו פעם אחת בשכמל"ו).
  - was: we might think one short response, like אמן, could cover all the blessings
  - now: we might think one response of praise, said once at the end, could cover all the blessings
- **Berakhot 63a, step 43** (hebrewStepName): Rejection is only for pushing aside a proof (rule 2); here the visitors answer חנינא's denunciation inside a story, which is not a proof, and the previous step is labelled מימרא (rule 3). Source: Berakhot 63a אמרו לו: כבר בנית ואי אתה יכול לסתור.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 63a, step 50** (hebrewStepName): 'והם שגרונו אצלך...' is the second half of the same reply begun in step 49 (גדיים שהנחת... והם שגרונו), and the message continues on 63b (ואמרו לאחינו שבגולה); a Conclusion is never a step in the middle (rule 5). Source: Berakhot 63a–b גדיים שהנחת נעשו תישים בעלי קרנים, והם שגרונו אצלך... ואמרו לאחינו שבגולה.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: תירוץ
- **Berakhot 64a, step 1** (hebrewStepName): The פסוקים complete רב יהודה בר זבידא's answer to 'מאי היא ברכה שברכו' (63b), so the step is the second half of a Response (rules 5 and 7). Source: Berakhot 63b–64a מאי היא ברכה שברכו? ... שנאמר פעלתי השמיני; Rashi s.v. שנאמר פעלתי השמיני.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: תשובה
- **Berakhot 64a, step 1** (title): Title names the step as a proof; it is the end of the answer begun on 63b. Source: Berakhot 64a.
  - was: Verses proving the blessing
  - now: Verses showing the blessing
- **Berakhot 64a, step 1** (stepSummary): The step completes an answer to a question, not a proof. Source: Berakhot 63b מאי היא ברכה שברכו.
  - was: finishes its proof from
  - now: finishes its answer from
- **Berakhot 64a, step 1** (whatsHappening): The step completes an answer to a question, not a proof. Source: Berakhot 63b מאי היא ברכה שברכו.
  - was: finishing a proof it started
  - now: finishing an answer it started
- **Berakhot 64a, step 7** (hebrewStepName): Rabba's and Rav Yosef's terms are part of the proof story for רבי אבין הלוי's teaching, and the story continues in the next step (אפילו אומנא לביתיה לא קרא); a Conclusion is never in the middle (rule 5). Source: Berakhot 64a מלך רבה עשרין ותרתין שנין... כל הנך שני דמלך רבה אפילו אומנא לביתיה לא קרא.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: ראיה
- **Berakhot 64a, step 13** (hebrewStepName): 'אלא לומר לך...' answers the difficulty 'וכי לפני אלהים אכלו' labelled קשיא in step 12; an answer to a Challenge is a Resolution (rule 2), as the site does for the same pattern on 63b steps 16-17. Source: Berakhot 64a וכי לפני אלהים אכלו? והלא לפני משה אכלו! אלא לומר לך.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: תירוץ

## 202611060301-rev102-berakhot-52-59

Approved: Drafted overnight Nov 5–6 2026 by Claude from a line-by-line check against the Gemara and Rashi on Sefaria (each finding re-checked before applying); David asked for fixes to go live overnight. Awaiting review by a rav.

- **Berakhot 52a, step 1** (phrases.1.notes): The ברייתא in question rules like בית הלל (והלכה כדברי בית הלל), not that one may follow בית שמאי; the note contradicts the step's own summary. Source: Berakhot 51b-52a: והלכה כדברי ב"ה, פשיטא דהא נפקא בת קול... ואיבעית אימא לאחר בת קול ורבי יהושע היא.
  - was: the ברייתא allowing one to follow בית שמאי follows his view
  - now: the ברייתא that rules like בית הלל even after the בת קול follows his view
- **Berakhot 52a, step 4** (hebrewStepName): Step 4 (לא סלקא דעתך, דקתני מאור ואחר כך בשמים) answers the שאלה of step 3 (ממאי דבית שמאי היא); rule 7: an answer to a Question is a Response even when it cites a source. Source: Berakhot 52a: והא ממאי דבית שמאי היא... לא סלקא דעתך; docs/step-labels.md rule 7.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: תשובה
- **Berakhot 52a, step 5** (hebrewStepName): Step 5 (דתניא אמר רבי יהודה) is the second half of the same answer as step 4; rule 5: the second half takes the first half's label. Source: Berakhot 52a: בית שמאי, דתניא אמר רבי יהודה...; docs/step-labels.md rules 5, 7.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: תשובה
- **Berakhot 52b, step 5** (hebrewStepName): דבר אחר: תכף לנטילת ידים סעודה is the ברייתא's second reason for בית הלל, not an answer to במאי קמיפלגי (the step itself says 'בית הלל add a second reason'); the parallel דבר אחר in step 20 is correctly מימרא. Source: Berakhot 52b: דבר אחר תכף לנטילת ידים סעודה. מאי דבר אחר? הכי קאמרי להו בית הלל לבית שמאי.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 53a, step 8** (hebrewStepName): Step 8 knocks out a suggested answer (וכי תימא... אלא הא דתניא המוציא שלהבת חייב), not a proof; rule 2: an objection to an answer or suggestion is a Challenge, and it quotes a ברייתא to attack (rule 1). Source: Berakhot 53a: וכי תימא הך איסורא אזל ליה... אלא הא דתניא המוציא שלהבת לרשות הרבים חייב; docs/step-labels.md rules 1-2.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Berakhot 53a, step 8** (title): Title names the kind; it must match the corrected label קשיא. Source: As above; docs/step-labels.md rule 2.
  - was: Rejection: carrying a flame
  - now: Challenge: carrying a flame
- **Berakhot 53b, step 38** (hebrewStepName): רבי נהוראי's words are the continuation of the ברייתא quoted with והתניא to attack 'חטוף ובריך'; rule 1: every step quoting the rest of an attacking source is קשיא (the step's own confusionAlert says it only strengthens the challenge). Source: Berakhot 53b: והתניא רבי יוסי אומר גדול העונה אמן... אמר ליה רבי נהוראי השמים כן הוא; docs/step-labels.md rule 1.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Berakhot 55a, step 2** (hebrewStepName): Rabbi Yitzchak's teaching is a second source brought to attack רב יהודה's praise of long prayer (the step itself says it strengthens the challenge), so under rule 1 it is a Challenge, not a Proof. Source: Berakhot 55a: וְאָמַר רַבִּי יִצְחָק ... וְעִיּוּן תְּפִלָּה ... לַשָּׁמַיִם! — answered by הָא לָא קַשְׁיָא; step-labels.md rule 1.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Berakhot 54b, step 35** (hebrewStepName): The step ends with an unanswered קַשְׁיָא and the thread closes there, so under rule 4 (a thread that ends without an answer) it is a Conclusion. Source: Berakhot 54b: וְאֵימָא בֵּי עַשְׂרָה שְׁאָר עַמָּא, וּתְרֵי רַבָּנַן! קַשְׁיָא; step-labels.md rules 4 and 9.
  - was: קשיא
  - now: מסקנא
- **Berakhot 55b, step 1** (hebrewStepName): This line finishes the answer to מנא לן? (55a step 39 שאלה / step 40 תשובה); nothing was challenged, and a second half takes the first half's label across the amud break. Source: Berakhot 55a–b: מְנָא לַן? — מִיּוֹסֵף ... וְהַהִיא שַׁעְתָּא אִמֵּיהּ לָא הֲוָת; step-labels.md rules 5 and 6.
  - was: תירוץ
  - now: תשובה
- **Berakhot 55b, step 7** (deeperAnalysis): The explanation misses the actual contradiction: Rashi says the difficulty is that Rav Huna has the good person seeing bad dreams, while the verse says bad dreams will not frighten him; 'too' makes it sound as if the two fit. Source: Berakhot 55b; Rashi s.v. ודוד לא ראה חלום טוב: קשיא לרב הונא דאמר לאדם טוב מראין לו חלום רע.
  - was: The earlier teaching left the good person without good dreams. This verse appears to protect such a person from frightening bad dreams too, so the גמרא needs a more precise account.
  - now: רב הונא's teaching implies that a good person is shown bad dreams instead of good ones (רש״י). This verse, as רב חסדא explains it, promises that bad dreams will not frighten such a person, so it seems to contradict רב הונא.
- **Berakhot 54a, step 29** (phrases.4.notes): Rashi explains that the wall of a house next to him fell and he went into the house, not that he hid inside the wall. Source: Berakhot 54a: אִיתְפָּרַקָא לֵיהּ אָשִׁיתָא, עָל לְגַוַּהּ; Rashi s.v. אתפרקא ליה אשיתא: נפלה חומת בית שהיה סמוך לו ונכנס לתוך הבית.
  - was: He was saved by hiding inside the wall.
  - now: רש״י: the wall of a nearby house fell, and he escaped into the house.
- **Berakhot 54b, step 42** (deeperAnalysis): The stated reason contradicts Rashi, who says the danger is from demons, attracted by weakened fortune (sick, birthing woman, mourner) or by jealousy (groom, bride, Torah scholars). Source: Berakhot 54b; Rashi s.v. שימור: מן המזיקין; s.v. חולה: שהורע מזלו; s.v. חתן וכלה ותלמידי חכמים: מקנאתו מתגרה בהם.
  - was: What these people share is that they are physically weak or their minds are elsewhere, so they need others to look out for them.
  - now: רש״י explains that they need guarding from מזיקין: a sick person, a חיה and an אבל because their מזל is weakened, and a חתן, a כלה and תלמידי חכמים because the מזיקין are jealous of them.
- **Berakhot 54b, step 42** (phrases.4.notes): The note gives a reason not in the text that differs from Rashi's (jealousy of the מזיקין). Source: Rashi s.v. חתן וכלה ותלמידי חכמים: מקנאתו מתגרה בהם.
  - was: They often walk alone at night to learn.
  - now: רש״י: the מזיקין attack them out of jealousy.
- **Berakhot 54b, step 41** (deeperAnalysis): Same issue as step 42: Rashi's reason is protection from demons (weakened מזל or jealousy), not a general weakened state; the phrase note of this same step already cites Rashi correctly. Source: Berakhot 54b; Rashi s.v. שימור: מן המזיקין; s.v. חתן וכלה: מקנאתו מתגרה בהם.
  - was: These people are in a sensitive or weakened state, so others should not leave them alone or unguarded.
  - now: רש״י explains that they need guarding from מזיקין.
- **Berakhot 56a, step 21** (deeperAnalysis): Rashi explains that the vav is properly absent in the verse (חסר), so its erasure does not invalidate the tefillin; the claim that the missing letter makes them invalid contradicts Rashi. Source: Berakhot 56a: וָאו דְּ״פֶטֶר חֲמוֹר״ וַדַּאי גְּהִיט; Rashi s.v. וי"ו דפטר חמור: והוא חסר וי"ו ובתפילין של רבא כתבו הכותב מלא ואח"כ מחקו.
  - was: רבא saw the phrase, but a single missing letter is easy to overlook. Even one missing letter makes תפילין invalid, so the warning still holds.
  - now: רבא saw the phrase, but a single erased letter is easy to overlook. According to רש״י, in the תורה the word חמר here is written without a ו; the scribe of רבא's תפילין wrote it with a ו and then erased it, so the ו is indeed erased.
- **Berakhot 56a, step 13** (hebrewStepName): A story event (the treasury break-in and Rava's arrest) is a Statement under rule 8; the Gemara does not bring it as a proof. Source: Berakhot 56a: לִמְחַר אִתְּבַר בֵּדַיְינָא דְּמַלְכָּא; docs/step-labels.md rule 8.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 56a, step 26** (hebrewStepName): Rava being struck twice is a line of the story, not a source brought as proof (rule 8). Source: Berakhot 56a: וּמְחוֹהוּ לְרָבָא תְּרֵי; docs/step-labels.md rule 8.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 56a, step 17** (whatsHappening): The barrel dreams do not end here: the next dream (step 18) is a barrel falling into a pit. Source: Berakhot 56a: חֲזַן חָבִיתָא דִּנְפַל לְבֵירָא.
  - was: finishing this set of barrel dreams
  - now: continuing this set of barrel dreams
- **Berakhot 56a, step 17** (relationToPreviousStep): A fifth barrel dream (the barrel in the pit) follows immediately. Source: Berakhot 56a: חֲזַן חָבִיתָא דִּנְפַל לְבֵירָא.
  - was: The fourth and final barrel-related dream in this run
  - now: The fourth barrel-related dream in this run
- **Berakhot 56a, step 17** (whatToRemember): There are five barrel dreams, not four; the barrel falling into a pit comes next. Source: Berakhot 56a: חֲזַן חָבִיתָא דִּנְפַל לְבֵירָא.
  - was: In all four barrel dreams (lettuce, meat, palm tree, pomegranate),
  - now: In these barrel dreams (lettuce, meat, palm tree, pomegranate, and next the barrel in the pit),
- **Berakhot 56b, step 2** (hebrewStepName): A story exchange (question and interpretation in one step) with no Question step before it; rule 6 says a Response needs a preceding Question, and rule 8 makes story lines Statements, as the parallel bar Hadaya exchanges on 56a are labelled. Source: Berakhot 56b: שָׁאַל בֶּן דָּמָא; docs/step-labels.md rules 6, 8.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 56b, step 3** (hebrewStepName): Story exchange with no Question step before it (rules 6, 8). Source: Berakhot 56b: אֲמַר לֵיהּ בַּר קַפָּרָא לְרַבִּי; docs/step-labels.md rules 6, 8.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 56b, step 4** (hebrewStepName): Story exchange with no Question step before it (rules 6, 8). Source: Berakhot 56b: אֲמַר לֵיהּ הָהוּא מִינָא; docs/step-labels.md rules 6, 8.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 56b, step 5** (hebrewStepName): Story exchange with no Question step before it (rules 6, 8). Source: Berakhot 56b: חֲזַאי דִּקְטִיף לִי כּוֹכְבָא; docs/step-labels.md rules 6, 8.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 56b, step 6** (hebrewStepName): Story exchange with no Question step before it (rules 6, 8). Source: Berakhot 56b: חֲזַאי עֵינַי דְּנָשְׁקָן; docs/step-labels.md rules 6, 8.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 56b, step 7** (hebrewStepName): Story exchange with no Question step before it (rules 6, 8). Source: Berakhot 56b: חֲזַאי דְּדָרֵיכְנָא בְּטוּנָא דְּאָסָא; docs/step-labels.md rules 6, 8.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 56b, step 8** (hebrewStepName): Story exchange with no Question step before it (rules 6, 8). Source: Berakhot 56b: חֲזַאי עוֹרְבֵי דְּהָדְרִי לְפוּרְיֵיהּ; docs/step-labels.md rules 6, 8.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 56b, step 9** (hebrewStepName): Story exchange with no Question step before it (rules 6, 8). Source: Berakhot 56b: חֲזַאי דְּנָקֵיטְנָא תְּרֵי יוֹנֵי; docs/step-labels.md rules 6, 8.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 56b, step 10** (hebrewStepName): Story exchange with no Question step before it (rules 6, 8). Source: Berakhot 56b: חֲזַאי דְּקָלֵיפְנָא בֵּיעֵי; docs/step-labels.md rules 6, 8.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 57a, step 44** (hebrewStepName): Abaye and Rava state their own view (once he went up, he went up); nothing was a proof, so it cannot be a Rejection (rule 2). It is a dissenting Statement. Source: Berakhot 57a: אַבָּיֵי וְרָבָא דְּאָמְרִי תַּרְוַויְיהוּ: כֵּיוָן שֶׁעָלָה — עָלָה; docs/step-labels.md rule 2.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 57a, step 56** (hebrewStepName): Rav Sheshet objects to the reciter's teaching (all the more so it doubled); an objection to a teaching is a Challenge, and Rejection is only for pushing aside a proof (rule 2). Source: Berakhot 57a: אֲמַר לֵיהּ רַב שֵׁשֶׁת: כׇּל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁנִּכְפְּלָה; docs/step-labels.md rule 2.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Berakhot 57a, step 57** (hebrewStepName): ולא היא answers Rav Sheshet's objection and keeps the reciter's teaching; an answer to a Challenge is a Resolution (rule 2), and no proof was pushed aside. Source: Berakhot 57a: וְלָא הִיא, רַב שֵׁשֶׁת הוּא דַּחֲזָא חִוְיָא; Rashi s.v. ולא היא; docs/step-labels.md rule 2.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: תירוץ
- **Berakhot 58b, step 18** (deeperAnalysis): The contradiction is answered on this same amud (לא קשיא: הא ממעי אמו...), so saying the passage stops before an answer is false and contradicts step 19. Source: Berakhot 58b: מיתיבי ... לא קשיא, הא ממעי אמו, הא בתר דאיתיליד.
  - was: This passage stops before an answer.
  - now: The answer comes in the next step.
- **Berakhot 59b** (overview): רבי אבהו only says when the rain blessing begins; the wording מודים אנחנו לך is רב יהודה's answer to מאי מברכין (and the ending is רבי יוחנן's). Source: Berakhot 59b: מאימתי מברכין על הגשמים... מאי מברכין? אמר רב יהודה: מודים אנחנו לך.
  - was: when רבי אבהו teaches a different rain blessing, מודים אנחנו לך, which ends
  - now: when רבי אבהו speaks of a different rain blessing, which רב יהודה gives as מודים אנחנו לך and which ends
- **Berakhot 58a, step 41** (phrases.1.notes): The ברייתא is not a source for רבי שילא's teaching; it is a different interpretation of the verse (as the step's own confusionAlert says). Source: Berakhot 58a: במתניתא תנא משמיה דרבי עקיבא: לך ה' הגדולה – זו קריעת ים סוף.
  - was: Here the גמרא brings a different, earlier source for this teaching.
  - now: Here the גמרא brings a different reading of the same verse, from a ברייתא.
- **Berakhot 58a, step 35** (hebrewStepName): This is a story event (רבי שילא kills the informer), and the passage continues with his דרשה; rulebook rules 5 and 8 make story lines Statements and forbid a Conclusion in the middle. Source: Berakhot 58a: מחייה בקולפא וקטליה; docs/step-labels.md rules 5, 8.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 58b, step 16** (hebrewStepName): The step is the closing event of a story (they set their eyes on him and he died), not the landing point of a discussion; rulebook rule 8 makes story lines Statements. Source: Berakhot 58b: יהבי ביה עינייהו ושכיב; docs/step-labels.md rule 8.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 58b, step 5** (hebrewStepName): No שאלה precedes this; עולא is countering רב חסדא's defence of his sighing (step 4, a תירוץ), and an objection to a defence is a Challenge (rules 2, 3, 6). Source: Berakhot 58b: אמר ליה: הכי אמר רבי יוחנן מיום שחרב בית המקדש...; docs/step-labels.md rules 2, 3, 6.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: קשיא
- **Berakhot 58b, step 6** (hebrewStepName): This is the second half of עולא's reply in step 5 and takes the same label (rule 5); in any case no שאלה precedes it (rule 6). Source: Berakhot 58b: ואמר רבי יוחנן עתיד הקב"ה להחזירן לישובן; docs/step-labels.md rules 5, 6.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: קשיא
- **Berakhot 59a, step 43** (hebrewStepName): In the West they object to the practice of falling on one's face, not to a proof; rulebook rule 2 keeps דחיה only for pushing aside a proof, so an objection like this is a Challenge (מימרא, an opposing ruling, would also be acceptable). Source: Berakhot 59a: לייטי עלה במערבא משום דמחזי כמאן דסגיד לקשתא; docs/step-labels.md rule 2.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: קשיא

## 202611060259-rev101-labels-sample-signals

Approved: Drafted overnight Nov 5–6 2026 by Claude from a line-by-line check against the Gemara and Rashi on Sefaria (each finding re-checked before applying); David asked for fixes to go live overnight. Awaiting review by a rav.

- **Nazir 40a, step 23** (hebrewStepName): The מה הצד derivation lands here, but the very next step (רבא מברניש: וליפרוך) attacks it, so by rule 5 it is not a Conclusion. It is the final link of the תשובה/דחיה chain that answers the source question, so it takes that chain's label. Source: Nazir 40a: וחזר הדין, לא ראי זה כראי זה, ולא ראי זה.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: תשובה
- **Megillah 5b, step 3** (hebrewStepName): The last line of the story (he applied 'two are better than one' to him). The challenge that follows is about רבי planting on פורים, not about this line. Rule 3: the rest of a story is Statement. Source: Megillah 5b: קרי עליה: ״טובים השנים מן האחד״..
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: מימרא
- **Yoma 15a, step 4** (hebrewStepName): 'Rather, what is the Torah saying? At the time of the lighting...' is the punchline of the proof in steps 2–3, and אבא שאול's reply (step 5) immediately pushes that proof aside. Rule 5: it takes the label of the proof it completes. Source: Yoma 15a: אלא מאי קאמר רחמנא — בעידן הדלקה תהא מקטר קטורת,.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: ראיה
- **Sanhedrin 100a, step 16** (hebrewStepName): The end of the story of the mocking student (רבי יוחנן's rebuke and punishment). The next challenge (giant people and the gates) is a new topic. Rule 3: story lines are Statement. Source: Sanhedrin 100a: אמר לו: ריקה! אם לא ראית לא האמנת? מלגלג על.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: מימרא
- **Shabbat 27b, step 6** (hebrewStepName): 'You might have thought like רבא; so it teaches us' restates the answer of step 3 after the aside explaining רבא (steps 4–5). It is the second half of that Resolution (rule 5). Source: Shabbat 27b: סלקא דעתך כדרבא קא משמע לן..
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: תירוץ
- **Rosh Hashanah 17b, step 1** (hebrewStepName): Second half of the drasha begun on 17a (step 29: 'for the remnant of His inheritance'), which is labelled מימרא. Rule 5: the second half takes the first half's label, even across the amud break. Source: Rosh Hashanah 17b: למי שמשים עצמו כשירים..
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: מימרא
- **Megillah 29b, step 4** (hebrewStepName): 'And since in ניסן we need offerings from the new collection, we read early...' finishes רבי טבי's answer (step 3) to the question of step 2. It is the second half of the Response (rule 5); the next challenge is about a different point. Source: Megillah 29b: וכיון דבניסן בעי אקרובי מתרומה חדשה, קדמינן וקרינן באחד באדר,.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: תשובה
- **Bava Metzia 16b, step 22** (hebrewStepName): The Gemara's elaboration of רבי אבהו's statement (steps 20–21: 'even with ratification, we worry it was paid'), immediately challenged by רבי ירמיה.   - now: דחיה
- **Niddah 9b, step 8** (hebrewStepName): 'And they do not disagree' (ולא פליגי) reconciles two answers about the length of an עונה. No challenge was raised, so it is not a Resolution. This follows the precedent in the Berakhot 38–39 batch. Source: Niddah 9b: ולא פליגי, מר קחשיב ימי טומאה וימי טהרה, ומר לא.
  - was: תירוץ
  - now: מימרא
- **Bava Kamma 114a, step 15** (hebrewStepName): 'He holds that giving up hope alone does not transfer ownership' is the Gemara explaining the reasoning of the ברייתא in step 14. No challenge was raised; it is an explanation (Statement). Source: Bava Kamma 114a: קסבר: יאוש כדי לא קני, ומעיקרא באיסורא אתא לידיה..
  - was: תירוץ
  - now: מימרא
- **Rosh Hashanah 6b, step 30** (hebrewStepName): 'And they do not disagree: one is about an unblemished firstborn...' reconciles אביי and רב אחא. No challenge was raised (ולא פליגי precedent). Source: Rosh Hashanah 6b: ולא פליגי: הא בתם,.
  - was: תירוץ
  - now: מימרא
- **Makkot 16b, step 16** (hebrewStepName): 'They do not disagree: big ants versus small ants' reconciles the two rulings. No challenge was raised (ולא פליגי precedent). Source: Makkot 16b: ולא פליגי, הא – ברברבי, והא – בזוטרי..
  - was: תירוץ
  - now: מימרא
- **Bava Batra 22b, step 8** (hebrewStepName): 'רבי יוסי בר חנינא said: they do not disagree: old city versus new city' reconciles רב and רבי אושעיא. No challenge was raised (ולא פליגי precedent). Source: Bava Batra 22b: אמר רבי יוסי בר חנינא: ולא פליגי – הא בעיר.
  - was: תירוץ
  - now: מימרא

## 202611060258-rev100-labels-discovery-b

Approved: Drafted overnight Nov 5–6 2026 by Claude from a line-by-line check against the Gemara and Rashi on Sefaria (each finding re-checked before applying); David asked for fixes to go live overnight. Awaiting review by a rav.

- **Moed Katan 12b, step 1** (hebrewStepName): Second half of אביי's statement that begins on 12a (נקטינן הלכות מועד כהלכות שבת ... יש מהן פטור אבל אסור). Not where a discussion lands; the second half takes the first half's label (12a step 31 is מימרא). Source: Moed Katan 12a-b: אָמַר אַבָּיֵי נָקְטִינַן הִלְכוֹת מוֹעֵד כְּהִלְכוֹת שַׁבָּת ... יֵשׁ מֵהֶן פָּטוּר אֲבָל אָסוּר.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: מימרא
- **Moed Katan 12b, step 26** (hebrewStepName): אביי cursing רב's ruling is an opposing statement, not the pushing aside of a proof. Nothing here is a proof. Source: Moed Katan 12b: לָיֵיט עֲלַהּ אַבָּיֵי.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: מימרא
- **Nazir 30a, step 1** (hebrewStepName): Second half of the challenge that ends 29b (אלא לרבי ... מאי איכא למימר): 'but he is still under his father's authority!' is an objection, not the pushing aside of a proof. 29b steps 12-13 are already קשיא. Source: Nazir 30a: הָא בִּרְשׁוּתֵיהּ דַּאֲבוּהּ קָאֵים.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Nazir 30a, step 4** (hebrewStepName): First half of a הניחא ... אלא challenge (הניחא לרבי יוסי ברבי יהודה ... אלא לרבי מאי איכא למימר); step 5 is its second half. It follows a קשיא, not a שאלה, so תשובה cannot stand. The same construction on 29b (steps 12-13) is labelled קשיא, קשיא. Source: Nazir 30a: הָנִיחָא לְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי בְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: קשיא
- **Nedarim 9a, step 11** (hebrewStepName): The ברייתא (טוב אשר לא תדור) is the source of step 10's difficulty: it shows neither רבי מאיר nor רבי יהודה distinguishes vow from gift. A source quoted to attack is a Challenge, never a Proof. Source: Nedarim 9a: דְּתַנְיָא טוֹב אֲשֶׁר לֹא תִדֹּר.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Niddah 52a, step 1** (hebrewStepName): 'ריחני' is the answer to 51b's last step (ולבני מערבא ... לאתויי מאי? לאתויי ריחני), which is labelled קשיא. It is the second half of an answer, not a conclusion. (If 51b step 30 is ever relabelled שאלה, like its twin step 28, this should be תשובה.) Source: Niddah 51b-52a: לְאַתּוֹיֵי מַאי ... רֵיחָנֵי.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: תירוץ
- **Niddah 52a, step 14** (hebrewStepName): רב כהנא brings the story of רבי ישמעאל's daughter to stop his colleagues' plan to rule like רבי יהודה. A source brought against a plan is a Challenge, not a Proof. Source: Niddah 52a: אֲמַר לְהוּ רַב כָּהֲנָא לֹא כָּךְ הָיָה מַעֲשֶׂה.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Niddah 52a, step 15** (hebrewStepName): Continuation of the same story רב כהנא quotes against the plan (rule 1: every step quoting the rest of the attacking source is קשיא). Source: Niddah 52a: אָמְרוּ דָּבָר שֶׁאָמַר אוֹתוֹ צַדִּיק.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: קשיא
- **Niddah 52a, step 17** (hebrewStepName): The vote (נמנו וגמרו) is the end of רב כהנא's precedent and is the point that defeats the plan; same source as step 14. Source: Niddah 52a: וְנִמְנוּ וְגָמְרוּ.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: קשיא
- **Niddah 52a, step 32** (hebrewStepName): Continues רבא's statement, which step 31 quotes (ואמר רבא) to attack two hairs in one follicle. Rule 1: the rest of the attacking source is also קשיא. Source: Niddah 52a: בִּסְעָרָה חֵירַף.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: קשיא
- **Niddah 52a, step 33** (hebrewStepName): Same as step 32: the second half of רבא's statement, quoted inside the challenge. Source: Niddah 52a: בִּסְעָרָה הֱשִׁיבוּהוּ.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: קשיא
- **Pesachim 15b, step 1** (hebrewStepName): End of the Tosefta that רבא quotes on 15a to object to רב נחמן (15a steps 13-16 are all קשיא). The second half takes the first half's label. Source: Pesachim 15b: הַפִּיגּוּל וְהַנּוֹתָר וְהַטָּמֵא בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: קשיא
- **Rosh Hashanah 32b, step 2** (hebrewStepName): No challenge comes before it. It is the Gemara's own comment on why the verse in step 1 is excluded, even though רב נחמן read it hopefully. Source: Rosh Hashanah 32b: וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאָמַר רַב נַחְמָן ... אַדְכּוֹרֵי רִיתְחָא בְּרֵישׁ שַׁתָּא לָא מַדְכְּרִינַן.
  - was: תירוץ
  - now: מימרא
- **Rosh Hashanah 32b, step 26** (hebrewStepName): The rest of the ברייתא רב הונא brings (תא שמע) to answer the challenge in step 24. Step 25 is תירוץ; the second half takes the first half's label. Source: Rosh Hashanah 32b: וְיָדַעְתָּ הַיּוֹם ... מַלְכוּת דִּבְרֵי רַבִּי יוֹסֵי.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: תירוץ
- **Sanhedrin 28a, step 8** (hebrewStepName): The second half of רבא's objection (step 7, ולטעמיך) to רמי בר חמא's reasoning, which answered the question in step 5. An objection to an answer is a Challenge. Source: Sanhedrin 28a: אֶלָּא הֲזָמָה מֵעָלְמָא קָאָתֵי.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Shabbat 59a, step 6** (hebrewStepName): Continuation of the attacking ברייתא brought with והתניא (steps 5 and 7, already קשיא in the data); the middle step must match. Source: Shabbat 59a: רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר אוֹמֵר בְּמִדְרָסוֹת.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: קשיא
- **Shevuot 4a, step 1** (hebrewStepName): Continuation of the משנה that 3b step 15 (תשובה) quotes as רבי יוחנן's source (אלא סתמא אשכח). The second half takes the first half's label. Source: Shevuot 4a: אֵינוֹ חַיָּיב אֶלָּא אַחַת. זוֹ הִיא שְׁבוּעַת בִּיטּוּי.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: תשובה
- **Shevuot 4a, step 14** (hebrewStepName): Objections (אי הכי ... ותו ... ותו) to the answer of steps 11-13 that the משנה is about lashes. An objection to an answer is a Challenge; it is answered by רב יוסף in step 15. Source: Shevuot 4a: אִי הָכִי שְׁתַּיִם שֶׁהֵן אַרְבַּע.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Shevuot 4a, step 14** (stepSummary): Summary calls the objections 'proofs'. Source: Shevuot 4a.
  - was: brings three proofs from the משנה that it cannot be about lashes
  - now: raises three objections from the משנה showing it cannot be about lashes
- **Shevuot 4a, step 16** (hebrewStepName): רב כהנא clarifies how to understand רב יוסף's answer; no proof is pushed aside. It is the first half of the statement that step 17 finishes (מימרא). Source: Shevuot 4a: לָא תֵּימָא רַבִּי נָסֵיב לַהּ אַלִּיבָּא דְתַנָּאֵי.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: מימרא
- **Sotah 22a, step 17** (hebrewStepName): Continuation of רבי יוחנן's statement quoted with אִינִי? וְהָאָמַר to attack the ברייתא (step 16). Rule 1: the rest of the attacking source is also קשיא. Source: Sotah 22a: יִרְאַת חֵטְא מִבְּתוּלָה.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Sotah 22a, step 18** (hebrewStepName): Same: the second story is part of the same attacking statement; answered in step 19. Source: Sotah 22a: קִיבּוּל שָׂכָר מֵאַלְמָנָה.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Sotah 22a, step 24** (hebrewStepName): Second half of רב's דרשה, quoted in step 23 (ראיה) to support רבי אבא. The second half takes the first half's label. Source: Sotah 22a: וַעֲצוּמִים כׇּל הֲרוּגֶיהָ.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: ראיה
- **Sukkah 13b, step 13** (hebrewStepName): Second half of a two-sided analysis (מאן דאמר קוצר ... מאן דאמר בוצר ...), and step 14 continues the discussion. It is not a conclusion. Source: Sukkah 13b: מַאן דְּאָמַר בּוֹצֵר שֶׁאֵין לוֹ יָדוֹת.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: מימרא
- **Taanit 26a, step 1** (hebrewStepName): End of אביי and רבא's answer that begins on 25b (step 30 תשובה): one says הלל only on a full stomach. Source: Taanit 25b-26a: לְפִי שֶׁאֵין אוֹמְרִים הַלֵּל אֶלָּא עַל נֶפֶשׁ שְׂבֵעָה.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: תשובה
- **Tamid 32a, step 27** (hebrewStepName): In a story, the king objects to the elders' answer. Rejection is only for pushing aside a proof; an objection to an answer is a Challenge. Source: Tamid 32a: דִּידִי טָבָא מִדִּידְכוּ.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Tamid 32a, step 27** (relationToPreviousStep): Matches the new label. Source: Tamid 32a.
  - was: Rejects the answer of step 26
  - now: Challenges the answer of step 26
- **Tamid 32a, step 36** (hebrewStepName): A line of the story in the middle of the episode (the king's next request follows at once). No Conclusion in the middle; story lines are Statement. Source: Tamid 32a: מִיָּד אַלְבֵּישׁ יָתְהוֹן לְבוּשִׁין דְּאַרְגְּווֹן.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: מימרא
- **Tamid 32a, step 37** (hebrewStepName): The king states a plan ('I want to go to אפריקי'); nothing is asked. Source: Tamid 32a: בָּעֵינָא דְּאֵיזִיל לִמְדִינַת אַפְרִיקֵי.
  - was: שאלה
  - now: מימרא
- **Tamid 32a, step 38** (hebrewStepName): The elders object to the king's plan ('you cannot go'). With no question before it, it cannot be a Response; an objection to a plan in a story is a Challenge. Source: Tamid 32a: לָא מָצֵית אָזְלַתְּ.
  - was: תשובה
  - now: קשיא
- **Temurah 2b, step 13** (hebrewStepName): End of the ברייתא that רבא quotes (תא שמע) in step 12 (ראיה); step 14 draws the inference. The second half takes the first half's label. Source: Temurah 2b: אָמַר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי בְּכוּלָּן אֲנִי רוֹאֶה לְהַחְמִיר.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: ראיה
- **Yoma 71a, step 1** (hebrewStepName): End of the ברייתא's answer on 70b (מאי טעמא בא? להוציא כף ומחתה, שכל הפרשה ...), which is labelled תשובה (70b step 29). Source: Yoma 71a: שֶׁכׇּל הַפָּרָשָׁה כּוּלָּהּ נֶאֶמְרָה עַל הַסֵּדֶר.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: תשובה
- **Yoma 71a, step 4** (hebrewStepName): Second half of רב חסדא's answer (step 3, תשובה): the reasoning that applies the tradition. Not a separate supporting source. Source: Yoma 71a: וְאִי אָמְרַתְּ כְּסִדְרָן כְּתִיבִי.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: תשובה
- **Yoma 71a, step 14** (hebrewStepName): אדרבה pushes back on אביי's answer (step 13, תירוץ) to the challenge of step 12. An objection to an answer is a Challenge; step 15 answers it (תירוץ). Source: Yoma 71a: אַדְּרַבָּה מָה שׂוֹרֵף דְּהַשְׁתָּא.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Yoma 71a, step 14** (relationToPreviousStep): Matches the new label; step 13 is an answer. Source: Yoma 71a.
  - was: Rejects אביי's proof in Step 13
  - now: Challenges אביי's answer in Step 13
- **Yoma 71a, step 16** (hebrewStepName): רבא's alternative answer to the challenge of step 12 (why change the verses and not the משנה), parallel to אביי's answer in step 13 (תירוץ). Source: Yoma 71a: רָבָא אָמַר אָמַר קְרָא יׇעֳמַד חַי.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: תירוץ
- **Yoma 71a, step 16** (title): Title should not name the step a proof once it is a Resolution. Source: Yoma 71a.
  - was: רבא's proof:
  - now: רבא's answer:
- **Yoma 71a, step 16** (relationToPreviousStep): Matches the new label. Source: Yoma 71a.
  - was: Gives an independent proof for the same point
  - now: Gives an independent answer for the same point
- **Zevachim 93b, step 28** (hebrewStepName): Inside the ברייתא that answers מנהני מילי (step 26); 'יכול ... תלמוד לומר' is a midrashic limit, not the pushing aside of a proof. The second half of the answer takes the first half's label (step 27, תשובה). Source: Zevachim 93b: יָכוֹל שֶׁאֲנִי מְרַבֶּה עוֹר עַד שֶׁלֹּא הוּפְשַׁט.
  - was: דחיה
  - now: תשובה

## 202611060257-rev99-berakhot-48-51-and-sample

Approved: Drafted overnight Nov 5–6 2026 by Claude from a line-by-line check against the Gemara and Rashi on Sefaria (each finding re-checked before applying); David asked for fixes to go live overnight. Awaiting review by a rav.

- **Berakhot 48a, step 4** (hebrewStepName): Rabbah's remark 'both of you will become Sages' is the last line of the Abaye/Rava story; no discussion lands here (the ruling like Rav Nachman was step 2), so per rule 3 the rest of a story is Statement. Source: Berakhot 48a: אֲמַר לְהוּ רַבָּה: תַּרְוַיְיכוּ רַבָּנַן הָוֵיתוּ; docs/step-labels.md rules 3-4.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 48b, step 1** (hebrewStepName): This is the continuation of the אִיתְּמַר נָמֵי supporting statement of Rava begun at 48a step 17 (labelled ראיה); the second half takes the first half's label, including across the amud break. Source: Berakhot 48a-b: אִיתְּמַר נָמֵי, אָמַר רַב חָנָא בַּר יְהוּדָה מִשְּׁמֵיהּ דְּרָבָא: אֲפִילּוּ לֹא / טִבֵּל עִמָּהֶם...; step-labels.md rule 5.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: ראיה
- **Berakhot 48b, step 2** (hebrewStepName): Still the same אִיתְּמַר נָמֵי supporting statement (its second clause, וּלְהוֹצִיא...עַד שֶׁיֹּאכַל כְּזַיִת דָּגָן), so it keeps the ראיה label of 48a step 17. Source: Berakhot 48b: וּלְהוֹצִיא אֶת הָרַבִּים יְדֵי חוֹבָתָם — אֵינוֹ מוֹצִיא עַד שֶׁיֹּאכַל כְּזַיִת דָּגָן; step-labels.md rule 5.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: ראיה
- **Berakhot 49a, step 1** (hebrewStepName): 'And this one was given with thirteen covenants' is just the second half of Pelimo's statement in the ברייתא (48b step 36, labelled מימרא); nothing is being proved, and a second half takes the first half's label. Source: Berakhot 48b-49a: פְּלֵימוֹ אוֹמֵר... שֶׁזּוֹ נִתְּנָה בְּשָׁלֹשׁ בְּרִיתוֹת, וְזוֹ נִתְּנָה בִּשְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה בְּרִיתוֹת; step-labels.md rule 5.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: מימרא
- **Berakhot 49b, step 14** (hebrewStepName): The משנה about holy meat (דִּתְנַן) is quoted to attack, showing Rabbi Meir and Rabbi Yehuda hold the reverse amounts (וְהָא אִיפְּכָא שָׁמְעִינַן לְהוּ); a source quoted to attack is a Challenge, never a Proof. Source: Berakhot 49b: וְהָא אִיפְּכָא שָׁמְעִינַן לְהוּ, דִּתְנַן: וְכֵן מִי שֶׁיָּצָא מִיְּרוּשָׁלַיִם...; step-labels.md rule 1.
  - was: ראיה
  - now: קשיא
- **Berakhot 49b, step 15** (hebrewStepName): Continuation of the same contradicting משנה (the reversed כביצה/כזית amounts are the punchline of the challenge), so it takes the Challenge label too. Rule 3 makes that a Challenge, and rule 6 does not allow a Response with no Question. Source: Berakhot 51b: לישדר מר כסא דברכתא לילתא. אמר ליה: הכי אמר רבי יוחנן...; docs/step-labels.md rules 3 and 6.

If the owner reads it as the closing line of the story, מימרא would also be acceptable. Source: Berakhot 51b: שלחה ליה: ממהדורי מילי ומסמרטוטי כלמי; docs/step-labels.md rules 2 and 3.

  - now: תירוץ

## 202611060252-rev97-berakhot-4

Approved: Drafted overnight Nov 5–6 2026 by Claude from a line-by-line check against the Gemara and Rashi on Sefaria (each finding re-checked before applying); David asked for fixes to go live overnight. Awaiting review by a rav.

- **Berakhot 40a, step 13** (deeperAnalysis): Rashi explains המקפה as drinking a lot of water after the meal, until the food floats, not while eating. Source: Berakhot 40a הַמַּקְפֶּה אֲכִילָתוֹ בְּמַיִם; Rashi s.v. המקפה אכילתו במים: שותה מים הרבה אחר אכילתו.
  - was: drinking plenty of water while eating protects the intestines
  - now: drinking plenty of water after eating protects the intestines
- **Berakhot 40a, step 41** (hebrewStepName): This is the middle of the ברייתא brought (דתניא) as the proof that רבי יהודה calls wheat a tree; it opens as ראיה in step 40, and a continuation takes the first half's label. Source: Berakhot 40a דְּתַנְיָא: אִילָן שֶׁאָכַל מִמֶּנּוּ אָדָם הָרִאשׁוֹן; rulebook rule 5.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: ראיה
- **Berakhot 40a, step 42** (hebrewStepName): רבי יהודה's line in the ברייתא is the actual proof for step 39 (the step itself says 'This completes the proof'), yet it is labelled Statement. Source: Berakhot 40a רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר: חִטָּה הָיְתָה; rulebook rule 5.
  - was: מימרא
  - now: ראיה
- **Berakhot 40b, step 1** (hebrewStepName): This is the second half of the קא משמע לן answer to the פשיטא challenge, which ends 40a as תירוץ (step 43); the second half takes the first half's label across the amud break. Source: Berakhot 40a-b הֵיכָא דְּכִי שָׁקְלַתְּ לֵיהּ לְפֵירֵי... אֲבָל הֵיכָא...; rulebook rule 5.
  - was: מסקנא
  - now: תירוץ

- **Berakhot 44b, step 37** (deeperAnalysis): Rashi explains 'the house' as the eater's belly (לכרס האוכל לפת), not a household where turnip is eaten often. Source: Berakhot 44b אוי לו לבית שהלפת עוברת בתוכו; Rashi s.v. אוי לבית: לכרס האוכל לפת.
  - was: having it pass through your house, meaning eating it regularly, is harmful.
  - now: according to רש״י the 'house' is the belly of the person who eats it, so turnip passing through it is harmful.
- **Berakhot 44b, step 37** (mimraCoreRuling): Same error as in deeperAnalysis: per Rashi the 'house' is the eater's belly. Source: Rashi Berakhot 44b s.v. אוי לבית.
  - was: Turnip is harmful to the home where it is eaten.
  - now: Turnip is harmful to the stomach of the one who eats it.
- **Berakhot 44b, step 37** (phrases.2.notes): The phrase note contradicts Rashi, who reads 'house' as the eater's belly. Source: Rashi Berakhot 44b s.v. אוי לבית: לכרס האוכל לפת.
  - was: Meaning a home where turnip is often eaten.
  - now: רש״י: the 'house' is the belly of the one who eats turnip.
